"메인 경로에서 dewarper 사용과 구성 반환값은 입력 복사본·출력 구성·보정값·기본 crop 전달을 어떻게 가릅니까?", "condition_display": "inline", "nodes": [{"id": "copy", "label": "센서와 ISP 구성이 성공하고 cfg가 메인 경로일 때 cfg를 ispCfg에 복사합니다.", "label_en": "After sensor and ISP configuration succeeds, copy the main-path cfg to ispCfg.", "shape": "box", "evidence": [{"source": "application/pipeline-configure", "sha256": "5b47ac903184e961b3b117b2c9e06dd6764b3a1b18d34ca7bb86035aa1c067c0", "quote": "int PipelineHandlerRkISP1::configure(Camera *camera, CameraConfiguration *c)\n{\n\tRkISP1CameraConfiguration *config =\n\t\tstatic_cast<RkISP1CameraConfiguration *>(c);\n\tRkISP1CameraData *data = cameraData(camera);\n\tCameraSensor *sensor = data->sensor_.get();\n\tint ret;\n\n\tret = initLinks(camera, *config);\n\tif (ret)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tconst PixelFormat &streamFormat = config->at(0).pixelFormat;\n\tconst PixelFormatInfo &info = PixelFormatInfo::info(streamFormat);\n\tisRaw_ = info.colourEncoding == PixelFormatInfo::ColourEncodingRAW;\n\tdata->usesDewarper_ = data->canUseDewarper_ && !isRaw_;\n\n\tTransform transform = config->combinedTransform();\n\tbool transposeAfterIsp = false;\n\tif (data->usesDewarper_) {\n\t\tif (!!(transform & Transform::Transpose))\n\t\t\ttransposeAfterIsp = true;\n\t\ttransform = Transform::Identity;\n\t}\n\n\t/*\n\t * Configure the format on the sensor output and propagate it through\n\t * the pipeline.\n\t */\n\tV4L2SubdeviceFormat format = config->sensorFormat();\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug) << \"Configuring sensor with \" << format;\n\n\tif (config->sensorConfig)\n\t\tret = sensor->applyConfiguration(*config->sensorConfig,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t transform,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t &format);\n\telse\n\t\tret = sensor->setFormat(&format, transform);\n\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug) << \"Sensor configured with \" << format;\n\n\t/* Propagate format through the internal media pipeline up to the ISP */\n\tret = data->pipe_.configure(sensor, &format);\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug) << \"Configuring ISP with : \" << format;\n\tret = isp_->setFormat(0, &format);\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tRectangle inputCrop(0, 0, format.size);\n\tret = isp_->setSelection(0, V4L2_SEL_TGT_CROP, &inputCrop);\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug)\n\t\t<< \"ISP input pad configured with \" << format\n\t\t<< \" crop \" << inputCrop;\n\n\tRectangle outputCrop = inputCrop;\n\n\t/* YUYV8_2X8 is required on the ISP source path pad for YUV output. */\n\tif (!isRaw_)\n\t\tformat.code = MEDIA_BUS_FMT_YUYV8_2X8;\n\n\t/*\n\t * On devices without DUAL_CROP (like the imx8mp) cropping needs to be\n\t * done on the ISP/IS output.\n\t *\n\t * If the dewarper is used, the cropping shall be done by the dewarper.\n\t */\n\tif (media_->hwRevision() == RKISP1_V_IMX8MP) {\n\t\t/* imx8mp has only a single path. */\n\t\tconst auto &cfg = config->at(0);\n\t\t/*\n\t\t * If the dewarper is used, all cropping including aspect ratio\n\t\t * preservation shall be done there. To ensure that the output\n\t\t * format provided by the ISP is supported by the dewarper, a\n\t\t * minimal crop still needs to be applied on the ISP output.\n\t\t *\n\t\t * \\todo It might be possible to allocate bigger buffers\n\t\t * (aligned to 8 pixels) with a stride matching format.size for\n\t\t * the ISP. The not-filled border could later be ignored by the\n\t\t * dewarper. This way we could skip the minimal crop here and\n\t\t * the MaximumScalerCrop would always match the isp output.\n\t\t */\n\t\tSize ispCrop;\n\t\tif (data->usesDewarper_)\n\t\t\tispCrop = dewarper_->adjustInputSize(cfg.pixelFormat,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t     format.size);\n\t\telse\n\t\t\tispCrop = format.size.boundedToAspectRatio(cfg.size)\n\t\t\t\t\t  .alignedUpTo(2, 2);\n\n\t\toutputCrop = ispCrop.centeredTo(Rectangle(format.size).center());\n\t\tformat.size = ispCrop;\n\t}\n\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug)\n\t\t<< \"Configuring ISP output pad with \" << format\n\t\t<< \" crop \" << outputCrop;\n\n\tret = isp_->setSelection(2, V4L2_SEL_TGT_CROP, &outputCrop);\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tformat.colorSpace = config->at(0).colorSpace;\n\tret = isp_->setFormat(2, &format);\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug)\n\t\t<< \"ISP output pad configured with \" << format\n\t\t<< \" crop \" << outputCrop;\n\n\tIPACameraSensorInfo sensorInfo;\n\tret = data->sensor_->sensorInfo(&sensorInfo);\n\tif (ret)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\t/* Apply the actual sensor crop for proper dewarp map calculation. */\n\tRectangle sensorCrop = outputCrop.transformedBetween(\n\t\tinputCrop, sensorInfo.analogCrop);\n\tif (data->usesDewarper_)\n\t\tdewarper_->setSensorCrop(sensorCrop);\n\tdata->properties_.set(properties::ScalerCropMaximum, sensorCrop);\n\n\tstd::map<unsigned int, IPAStream> streamConfig;\n\n\tfor (const StreamConfiguration &cfg : *config) {\n\t\tif (cfg.stream() == &data->mainPathStream_) {\n\t\t\t/*\n\t\t\t * To allow for digital zoom, scaling down should happen\n\t\t\t * in the dewarper, instead of the resizer. Configure\n\t\t\t * the isp output to the same size as the sensor output.\n\t\t\t */\n\t\t\tStreamConfiguration ispCfg = cfg;\n\t\t\tif (data->usesDewarper_) {\n\t\t\t\tispCfg.bufferCount = kRkISP1MinBufferCount;\n\t\t\t\tispCfg.size = format.size;\n\t\t\t\tispCfg.stride =\n\t\t\t\t\tPixelFormatInfo::info(ispCfg.pixelFormat)\n\t\t\t\t\t\t.stride(ispCfg.size.width, 0);\n\n\t\t\t\tret = dewarper_->configure(ispCfg, { cfg }, data->dewarpParams_);\n\t\t\t\tif (ret)\n\t\t\t\t\treturn ret;\n\n\t\t\t\tdewarper_->setTransform(cfg.stream(), config->combinedTransform());\n\t\t\t\t/*\n\t\t\t\t * Apply a default scaler crop that keeps the\n\t\t\t\t * aspect ratio.\n\t\t\t\t */\n\t\t\t\tSize size = cfg.size;\n\t\t\t\tif (transposeAfterIsp)\n\t\t\t\t\tsize.transpose();\n\t\t\t\tsize = sensorCrop.size().boundedToAspectRatio(size);\n\n\t\t\t\tControlList ctrls;\n\t\t\t\tctrls.set(controls::ScalerCrop, size.centeredTo(sensorCrop.center()));\n\t\t\t\tdewarper_->setControls(cfg.stream(), ctrls);\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tret = mainPath_.configure(ispCfg, format);\n\t\t\tstreamConfig[0] = IPAStream(cfg.pixelFormat,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t    cfg.size);\n\t\t} else if (hasSelfPath_) {\n\t\t\tret = selfPath_.configure(cfg, format);\n\t\t\tstreamConfig[1] = IPAStream(cfg.pixelFormat,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t    cfg.size);\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\treturn -ENODEV;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (ret)\n\t\t\treturn ret;\n\t}\n\n\tV4L2DeviceFormat paramFormat;\n\tparamFormat.fourcc = V4L2PixelFormat(V4L2_META_FMT_RK_ISP1_EXT_PARAMS);\n\tret = param_->setFormat(&paramFormat);\n\tif (ret)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tV4L2DeviceFormat statFormat;\n\tstatFormat.fourcc = V4L2PixelFormat(V4L2_META_FMT_RK_ISP1_STAT_3A);\n\tret = stat_->setFormat(&statFormat);\n\tif (ret)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\t/* Inform IPA of stream configuration and sensor controls. */\n\tipa::rkisp1::IPAConfigInfo ipaConfig{ sensorInfo,\n\t\t\t\t\t      data->sensor_->controls(),\n\t\t\t\t\t      paramFormat.fourcc };\n\n\tret = data->ipa_->configure(ipaConfig, streamConfig, &data->ipaControls_);\n\tif (ret) {\n\t\tLOG(RkISP1, Error) << \"failed configuring IPA (\" << ret << \")\";\n\t\treturn ret;\n\t}\n\n\treturn updateControls(data);\n}"}]}, {"id": "use_dewarper", "label": "기존 usesDewarper_ 값을 검사합니다. 이 값은 앞서 canUseDewarper_가 참이고 isRaw_가 거짓일 때 참으로 설정되었습니다.", "label_en": "Check the existing usesDewarper_ value, set earlier to true when canUseDewarper_ is true and "ispCfg의 bufferCount를 kRkISP1MinBufferCount로, size를 format.size로 설정하고 stride를 다시 계산합니다.", "label_en": "Set ispCfg bufferCount to kRkISP1MinBufferCount and size to format.size, then recalculate stride.", "shape": "box", "evidence": [{"source": "application/pipeline-configure", "sha256": "5b47ac903184e961b3b117b2c9e06dd6764b3a1b18d34ca7bb86035aa1c067c0", "quote": "int PipelineHandlerRkISP1::configure(Camera *camera, CameraConfiguration *c)\n{\n\tRkISP1CameraConfiguration *config =\n\t\tstatic_cast<RkISP1CameraConfiguration *>(c);\n\tRkISP1CameraData *data = cameraData(camera);\n\tCameraSensor *sensor = data->sensor_.get();\n\tint ret;\n\n\tret = initLinks(camera, *config);\n\tif (ret)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tconst PixelFormat &streamFormat = config->at(0).pixelFormat;\n\tconst PixelFormatInfo &info = PixelFormatInfo::info(streamFormat);\n\tisRaw_ = info.colourEncoding == PixelFormatInfo::ColourEncodingRAW;\n\tdata->usesDewarper_ = data->canUseDewarper_ && !isRaw_;\n\n\tTransform transform = config->combinedTransform();\n\tbool transposeAfterIsp = false;\n\tif (data->usesDewarper_) {\n\t\tif (!!(transform & Transform::Transpose))\n\t\t\ttransposeAfterIsp = true;\n\t\ttransform = Transform::Identity;\n\t}\n\n\t/*\n\t * Configure the format on the sensor output and propagate it through\n\t * the pipeline.\n\t */\n\tV4L2SubdeviceFormat format = config->sensorFormat();\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug) << \"Configuring sensor with \" << format;\n\n\tif (config->sensorConfig)\n\t\tret = sensor->applyConfiguration(*config->sensorConfig,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t transform,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t &format);\n\telse\n\t\tret = sensor->setFormat(&format, transform);\n\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug) << \"Sensor configured with \" << format;\n\n\t/* Propagate format through the internal media pipeline up to the ISP */\n\tret = data->pipe_.configure(sensor, &format);\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug) << \"Configuring ISP with : \" << format;\n\tret = isp_->setFormat(0, &format);\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tRectangle inputCrop(0, 0, format.size);\n\tret = isp_->setSelection(0, V4L2_SEL_TGT_CROP, &inputCrop);\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug)\n\t\t<< \"ISP input pad configured with \" << format\n\t\t<< \" crop \" << inputCrop;\n\n\tRectangle outputCrop = inputCrop;\n\n\t/* YUYV8_2X8 is required on the ISP source path pad for YUV output. */\n\tif (!isRaw_)\n\t\tformat.code = MEDIA_BUS_FMT_YUYV8_2X8;\n\n\t/*\n\t * On devices without DUAL_CROP (like the imx8mp) cropping needs to be\n\t * done on the ISP/IS output.\n\t *\n\t * If the dewarper is used, the cropping shall be done by the dewarper.\n\t */\n\tif (media_->hwRevision() == RKISP1_V_IMX8MP) {\n\t\t/* imx8mp has only a single path. */\n\t\tconst auto &cfg = config->at(0);\n\t\t/*\n\t\t * If the dewarper is used, all cropping including aspect ratio\n\t\t * preservation shall be done there. To ensure that the output\n\t\t * format provided by the ISP is supported by the dewarper, a\n\t\t * minimal crop still needs to be applied on the ISP output.\n\t\t *\n\t\t * \\todo It might be possible to allocate bigger buffers\n\t\t * (aligned to 8 pixels) with a stride matching format.size for\n\t\t * the ISP. The not-filled border could later be ignored by the\n\t\t * dewarper. This way we could skip the minimal crop here and\n\t\t * the MaximumScalerCrop would always match the isp output.\n\t\t */\n\t\tSize ispCrop;\n\t\tif (data->usesDewarper_)\n\t\t\tispCrop = dewarper_->adjustInputSize(cfg.pixelFormat,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t     format.size);\n\t\telse\n\t\t\tispCrop = format.size.boundedToAspectRatio(cfg.size)\n\t\t\t\t\t  .alignedUpTo(2, 2);\n\n\t\toutputCrop = ispCrop.centeredTo(Rectangle(format.size).center());\n\t\tformat.size = ispCrop;\n\t}\n\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug)\n\t\t<< \"Configuring ISP output pad with \" << format\n\t\t<< \" crop \" << outputCrop;\n\n\tret = isp_->setSelection(2, V4L2_SEL_TGT_CROP, &outputCrop);\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tformat.colorSpace = config->at(0).colorSpace;\n\tret = isp_->setFormat(2, &format);\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug)\n\t\t<< \"ISP output pad configured with \" << format\n\t\t<< \" crop \" << outputCrop;\n\n\tIPACameraSensorInfo sensorInfo;\n\tret = data->sensor_->sensorInfo(&sensorInfo);\n\tif (ret)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\t/* Apply the actual sensor crop for proper dewarp map calculation. */\n\tRectangle sensorCrop = outputCrop.transformedBetween(\n\t\tinputCrop, sensorInfo.analogCrop);\n\tif (data->usesDewarper_)\n\t\tdewarper_->setSensorCrop(sensorCrop);\n\tdata->properties_.set(properties::ScalerCropMaximum, sensorCrop);\n\n\tstd::map<unsigned int, IPAStream> streamConfig;\n\n\tfor (const StreamConfiguration &cfg : *config) {\n\t\tif (cfg.stream() == &data->mainPathStream_) {\n\t\t\t/*\n\t\t\t * To allow for digital zoom, scaling down should happen\n\t\t\t * in the dewarper, instead of the resizer. Configure\n\t\t\t * the isp output to the same size as the sensor output.\n\t\t\t */\n\t\t\tStreamConfiguration ispCfg = cfg;\n\t\t\tif (data->usesDewarper_) {\n\t\t\t\tispCfg.bufferCount = kRkISP1MinBufferCount;\n\t\t\t\tispCfg.size = format.size;\n\t\t\t\tispCfg.stride =\n\t\t\t\t\tPixelFormatInfo::info(ispCfg.pixelFormat)\n\t\t\t\t\t\t.stride(ispCfg.size.width, 0);\n\n\t\t\t\tret = dewarper_->configure(ispCfg, { cfg }, data->dewarpParams_);\n\t\t\t\tif (ret)\n\t\t\t\t\treturn ret;\n\n\t\t\t\tdewarper_->setTransform(cfg.stream(), config->combinedTransform());\n\t\t\t\t/*\n\t\t\t\t * Apply a default scaler crop that keeps the\n\t\t\t\t * aspect ratio.\n\t\t\t\t */\n\t\t\t\tSize size = cfg.size;\n\t\t\t\tif (transposeAfterIsp)\n\t\t\t\t\tsize.transpose();\n\t\t\t\tsize = sensorCrop.size().boundedToAspectRatio(size);\n\n\t\t\t\tControlList ctrls;\n\t\t\t\tctrls.set(controls::ScalerCrop, size.centeredTo(sensorCrop.center()));\n\t\t\t\tdewarper_->setControls(cfg.stream(), ctrls);\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tret = mainPath_.configure(ispCfg, format);\n\t\t\tstreamConfig[0] = IPAStream(cfg.pixelFormat,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t    cfg.size);\n\t\t} else if (hasSelfPath_) {\n\t\t\tret = selfPath_.configure(cfg, format);\n\t\t\tstreamConfig[1] = IPAStream(cfg.pixelFormat,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t    cfg.size);\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\treturn -ENODEV;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (ret)\n\t\t\treturn ret;\n\t}\n\n\tV4L2DeviceFormat paramFormat;\n\tparamFormat.fourcc = V4L2PixelFormat(V4L2_META_FMT_RK_ISP1_EXT_PARAMS);\n\tret = param_->setFormat(&paramFormat);\n\tif (ret)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tV4L2DeviceFormat statFormat;\n\tstatFormat.fourcc = V4L2PixelFormat(V4L2_META_FMT_RK_ISP1_STAT_3A);\n\tret = stat_->setFormat(&statFormat);\n\tif (ret)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\t/* Inform IPA of stream configuration and sensor controls. */\n\tipa::rkisp1::IPAConfigInfo ipaConfig{ sensorInfo,\n\t\t\t\t\t      data->sensor_->controls(),\n\t\t\t\t\t      paramFormat.fourcc };\n\n\tret = data->ipa_->configure(ipaConfig, streamConfig, &data->ipaControls_);\n\tif (ret) {\n\t\tLOG(RkISP1, Error) << \"failed configuring IPA (\" << ret << \")\";\n\t\treturn ret;\n\t}\n\n\treturn updateControls(data);\n}"}]}, {"id": "dewarp_configure", kRkISP1MinBufferCount;\n\t\t\t\tispCfg.size = format.size;\n\t\t\t\tispCfg.stride =\n\t\t\t\t\tPixelFormatInfo::info(ispCfg.pixelFormat)\n\t\t\t\t\t\t.stride(ispCfg.size.width, 0);\n\n\t\t\t\tret = dewarper_->configure(ispCfg, { cfg }, data->dewarpParams_);\n\t\t\t\tif (ret)\n\t\t\t\t\treturn ret;\n\n\t\t\t\tdewarper_->setTransform(cfg.stream(), size.centeredTo(sensorCrop.center()));\n\t\t\t\tdewarper_->setControls(cfg.stream(), ctrls);\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tret = mainPath_.configure(ispCfg, format);\n\t\t\tstreamConfig[0] = IPAStream(cfg.pixelFormat,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t    cfg.size);\n\t\t} else if (hasSelfPath_) {\n\t\t\tret = selfPath_.configure(cfg, format);\n\t\t\tstreamConfig[1] = IPAStream(cfg.pixelFormat,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t    cfg.size);\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\treturn -ENODEV;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (ret)\n\t\t\treturn ret;\n\t}\n\n\tV4L2DeviceFormat paramFormat;\n\tparamFormat.fourcc = V4L2PixelFormat(V4L2_META_FMT_RK_ISP1_EXT_PARAMS);\n\tret = param_->setFormat(&paramFormat);\n\tif (ret)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tV4L2DeviceFormat statFormat;\n\tstatFormat.fourcc = V4L2PixelFormat(V4L2_META_FMT_RK_ISP1_STAT_3A);\n\tret = stat_->setFormat(&statFormat);\n\tif (ret)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\t/* Inform IPA of stream configuration and sensor controls. the sensor output and propagate it through\n\t * the pipeline.\n\t */\n\tV4L2SubdeviceFormat format = config->sensorFormat();\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug) << \"Configuring sensor with \" << format;\n\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug) << \"Sensor configured with \" << format;\n\n\t/* Propagate format through the internal media pipeline up to the ISP */\n\tret = data->pipe_.configure(sensor, &format);\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug) << \"Configuring ISP with : \" << format;\n\tret = isp_->setFormat(0, &format);\n\tif (ret < input pad configured with \" << format\n\t\t<< \" crop \" << inputCrop;\n\n\tRectangle outputCrop = inputCrop;\n\n\t/* YUYV8_2X8 is required on the ISP source path pad for YUV output. */\n\tif (!isRaw_)\n\t\tformat.code = MEDIA_BUS_FMT_YUYV8_2X8;\n\n\t/*\n\t * On devices without DUAL_CROP (like the imx8mp) cropping needs to be\n\t * done on the ISP/IS output.\n\t *\n\t * If the dewarper is * If the dewarper is used, all cropping including aspect ratio\n\t\t * preservation shall be done there. The not-filled border could later be ignored by the\n\t\t * dewarper. This way we could skip the minimal crop here and\n\t\t * the MaximumScalerCrop would always match the isp output.\n\t\t */\n\t\tSize ispCrop;\n\t\tif (data->usesDewarper_)\n\t\t\tispCrop = dewarper_->adjustInputSize(cfg.pixelFormat,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t     format.size);\n\t\telse\n\t\t\tispCrop = format.size.boundedToAspectRatio(cfg.size)\n\t\t\t\t\t  .alignedUpTo(2, 2);\n\n\t\toutputCrop = ispCrop.centeredTo(Rectangle(format.size).center());\n\t\tformat.size = ispCrop;\n\t}\n\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug)\n\t\t<< \"Configuring ISP output pad with \" << format\n\t\t<< \" crop \" << outputCrop;\n\n\tret = isp_->setSelection(2, V4L2_SEL_TGT_CROP, &outputCrop);\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tformat.colorSpace = config->at(0).colorSpace;\n\tret = isp_->setFormat(2, &format);\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug)\n\t\t<< \"ISP output pad configured with \" << format\n\t\t<< \" crop \" << outputCrop;\n\n\tIPACameraSensorInfo sensorInfo;\n\tret = data->sensor_->sensorInfo(&sensorInfo);\n\tif (ret)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\t/* Apply the actual sensor crop for proper dewarp map calculation. */\n\tRectangle sensorCrop = outputCrop.transformedBetween(\n\t\tinputCrop, sensorInfo.analogCrop);\n\tif (data->usesDewarper_)\n\t\tdewarper_->setSensorCrop(sensorCrop);\n\tdata->properties_.set(properties::ScalerCropMaximum, sensorCrop);\n\n\tstd::map<unsigned int, IPAStream> streamConfig;\n\n\tfor (const StreamConfiguration &cfg : *config) {\n\t\tif (cfg.stream() == &data->mainPathStream_) {\n\t\t\t/*\n\t\t\t * To allow for digital zoom, scaling down should happen\n\t\t\t * in the dewarper, instead of the resizer. kRkISP1MinBufferCount;\n\t\t\t\tispCfg.size = format.size;\n\t\t\t\tispCfg.stride =\n\t\t\t\t\tPixelFormatInfo::info(ispCfg.pixelFormat)\n\t\t\t\t\t\t.stride(ispCfg.size.width, 0);\n\n\t\t\t\tret = dewarper_->configure(ispCfg, { cfg }, data->dewarpParams_);\n\t\t\t\tif (ret)\n\t\t\t\t\treturn ret;\n\n\t\t\t\tdewarper_->setTransform(cfg.stream(), config->combinedTransform());\n\t\t\t\t/*\n\t\t\t\t * Apply a default scaler crop that keeps the\n\t\t\t\t * aspect ratio.\n\t\t\t\t */\n\t\t\t\tSize size = cfg.size;\n\t\t\t\tif (transposeAfterIsp)\n\t\t\t\t\tsize.transpose();\n\t\t\t\tsize = sensorCrop.size().boundedToAspectRatio(size);\n\n\t\t\t\tControlList ctrls;\n\t\t\t\tctrls.set(controls::ScalerCrop, size.centeredTo(sensorCrop.center()));\n\t\t\t\tdewarper_->setControls(cfg.stream(), ctrls);\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tret = mainPath_.configure(ispCfg, format);\n\t\t\tstreamConfig[0] =   cfg.size);\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\treturn -ENODEV;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (ret)\n\t\t\treturn ret;\n\t}\n\n\tV4L2DeviceFormat paramFormat;\n\tparamFormat.fourcc = = false;\n\tif (data->usesDewarper_) {\n\t\tif (!!(transform & Transform::Transpose))\n\t\t\ttransposeAfterIsp = true;\n\t\ttransform = Transform::Identity;\n\t}\n\n\t/*\n\t * Configure the format on the sensor output and propagate it through\n\t * the pipeline.\n\t */\n\tV4L2SubdeviceFormat format = config->sensorFormat();\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug) << \"Configuring sensor with \" << format;\n\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug) << \"Sensor configured with \" << format;\n\n\t/* Propagate format through the internal media pipeline up to the ISP */\n\tret = data->pipe_.configure(sensor, &format);\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug) << \"Configuring ISP with : \" << format;\n\tret = isp_->setFormat(0, &format);\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tRectangle inputCrop(0, 0, format.size);\n\tret = isp_->setSelection(0, V4L2_SEL_TGT_CROP, &inputCrop);\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug)\n\t\t<< \"ISP input pad configured with \" << format\n\t\t<< \" crop \" << inputCrop;\n\n\tRectangle outputCrop = inputCrop;\n\n\t/* YUYV8_2X8 is required on the ISP source path pad for YUV output. */\n\tif (!isRaw_)\n\t\tformat.code = MEDIA_BUS_FMT_YUYV8_2X8;\n\n\t/*\n\t * On devices without DUAL_CROP (like the imx8mp) cropping needs to be\n\t * done on the ISP/IS output.\n\t *\n\t * If the dewarper is used, the cropping shall be done by the dewarper.\n\t */\n\tif (media_->hwRevision() == RKISP1_V_IMX8MP) {\n\t\t/* imx8mp has only a single path. */\n\t\tconst auto &cfg = config->at(0);\n\t\t/*\n\t\t * If the dewarper is used, all cropping including aspect ratio\n\t\t * preservation shall be done there. To ensure that the output\n\t\t * format provided by the ISP is supported by the dewarper, a\n\t\t * minimal crop still needs to be applied on the ISP output.\n\t\t *\n\t\t * \\todo It might be possible to allocate bigger buffers\n\t\t * (aligned to 8 pixels) with a stride matching format.size for\n\t\t * the ISP. The not-filled border could later be ignored by the\n\t\t * dewarper. This way we could skip the minimal crop here and\n\t\t * the MaximumScalerCrop would always match the isp output.\n\t\t */\n\t\tSize ispCrop;\n\t\tif (data->usesDewarper_)\n\t\t\tispCrop = dewarper_->adjustInputSize(cfg.pixelFormat,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t     format.size);\n\t\telse\n\t\t\tispCrop = format.size.boundedToAspectRatio(cfg.size)\n\t\t\t\t\t  .alignedUpTo(2, 2);\n\n\t\toutputCrop = ispCrop.centeredTo(Rectangle(format.size).center());\n\t\tformat.size = ispCrop;\n\t}\n\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug)\n\t\t<< \"Configuring ISP output pad with \" << format\n\t\t<< \" crop \" << outputCrop;\n\n\tret = isp_->setSelection(2, V4L2_SEL_TGT_CROP, &outputCrop);\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tformat.colorSpace = config->at(0).colorSpace;\n\tret = isp_->setFormat(2, &format);\n\tif (ret < 0)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tLOG(RkISP1, Debug)\n\t\t<< \"ISP output pad configured with \" << format\n\t\t<< \" crop \" << outputCrop;\n\n\tIPACameraSensorInfo sensorInfo;\n\tret = data->sensor_->sensorInfo(&sensorInfo);\n\tif (ret)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\t/* Apply the actual sensor crop for proper dewarp map calculation. */\n\tRectangle sensorCrop = outputCrop.transformedBetween(\n\t\tinputCrop, sensorInfo.analogCrop);\n\tif (data->usesDewarper_)\n\t\tdewarper_->setSensorCrop(sensorCrop);\n\tdata->properties_.set(properties::ScalerCropMaximum, sensorCrop);\n\n\tstd::map<unsigned int, IPAStream> streamConfig;\n\n\tfor (const StreamConfiguration &cfg : *config) {\n\t\tif (cfg.stream() == &data->mainPathStream_) {\n\t\t\t/*\n\t\t\t * To allow for digital zoom, scaling down should happen\n\t\t\t * in the dewarper, instead of the resizer. Configure\n\t\t\t * the isp output to the same size as the sensor output.\n\t\t\t */\n\t\t\tStreamConfiguration ispCfg = cfg;\n\t\t\tif (data->usesDewarper_) {\n\t\t\t\tispCfg.bufferCount = kRkISP1MinBufferCount;\n\t\t\t\tispCfg.size = format.size;\n\t\t\t\tispCfg.stride =\n\t\t\t\t\tPixelFormatInfo::info(ispCfg.pixelFormat)\n\t\t\t\t\t\t.stride(ispCfg.size.width, 0);\n\n\t\t\t\tret = dewarper_->configure(ispCfg, { cfg }, data->dewarpParams_);\n\t\t\t\tif (ret)\n\t\t\t\t\treturn ret;\n\n\t\t\t\tdewarper_->setTransform(cfg.stream(), config->combinedTransform());\n\t\t\t\t/*\n\t\t\t\t * Apply a default scaler crop that keeps the\n\t\t\t\t * aspect ratio.\n\t\t\t\t */\n\t\t\t\tSize size = cfg.size;\n\t\t\t\tif (transposeAfterIsp)\n\t\t\t\t\tsize.transpose();\n\t\t\t\tsize = sensorCrop.size().boundedToAspectRatio(size);\n\n\t\t\t\tControlList ctrls;\n\t\t\t\tctrls.set(controls::ScalerCrop, size.centeredTo(sensorCrop.center()));\n\t\t\t\tdewarper_->setControls(cfg.stream(), ctrls);\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tret = mainPath_.configure(ispCfg, format);\n\t\t\tstreamConfig[0] = IPAStream(cfg.pixelFormat,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t    cfg.size);\n\t\t} else if (hasSelfPath_) {\n\t\t\tret = selfPath_.configure(cfg, format);\n\t\t\tstreamConfig[1] = IPAStream(cfg.pixelFormat,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t    cfg.size);\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\treturn -ENODEV;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (ret)\n\t\t\treturn ret;\n\t}\n\n\tV4L2DeviceFormat paramFormat;\n\tparamFormat.fourcc = V4L2PixelFormat(V4L2_META_FMT_RK_ISP1_EXT_PARAMS);\n\tret = param_->setFormat(&paramFormat);\n\tif (ret)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\tV4L2DeviceFormat statFormat;\n\tstatFormat.fourcc = V4L2PixelFormat(V4L2_META_FMT_RK_ISP1_STAT_3A);\n\tret = stat_->setFormat(&statFormat);\n\tif (ret)\n\t\treturn ret;\n\n\t/* Inform IPA of stream configuration and sensor controls. # RKISP1 카메라별 왜곡 보정

**카메라별 보정값의 보관 위치와 설정 시점을 확인한 뒤 튜닝 입력과 커널 지원을 점검하세요.**

| 지금 확인할 내용 | 이동할 절 |
|---|---|
| `RkISP1CameraData`는 카메라별 `dewarpParams_`를 `std::optional<Dw100VertexMap::DewarpParams>` 멤버로 보관합니다. | [카메라별 보정값의 소유권](#카메라별-보정값의-소유권) |
| `ConverterDW100Module::loadDewarpParams`는 먼저 `dewarpParams`를 비웁니다. | [튜닝 입력의 기본값과 오류](#튜닝-입력의-기본값과-오류) |
| `ConverterDW100Module::configure`는 각 출력 설정을 순회하며 해당 스트림의 `vertexMap`에 `inputCfg.size`를 `setInputSize`로, `outputCfg.size`를 `setOutputSize`로, `sensorCrop_`을 `setSensorCrop`으로 순서대로 전달합니다. | [스트림 적용과 실행 확인 범위](#스트림-적용과-실행-확인-범위) |

## 카메라별 보정값의 소유권

`RkISP1CameraData`는 카메라별 `dewarpParams_`를 `std::optional<Dw100VertexMap::DewarpParams>` 멤버로 보관합니다. `RkISP1CameraData::loadTuningFile()`은 `modules` 목록에서 `Dewarp` 항목을 찾아 `loadDewarpParams()`에 이 멤버를 전달하고, 성공하면 `canUseDewarper_`를 참으로 설정한 뒤 0을 반환합니다. 적재 함수가 0이 아닌 값을 반환하면 그 값을 그대로 반환합니다. `src/libcamera/pipeline/rkisp1/rkisp1.cpp:94`, `src/libcamera/pipeline/rkisp1/rkisp1.cpp:434`

<!-- sdd:visual:per-camera-parameters -->

??? note "그림 근거와 확인 범위"
    선택한 카메라의 선택적 보정값은 어디에 보관되고 튜닝 항목에서 어떻게 채워질 수 있으며, 어떤 조건에서 보정기에 전달됩니까?
    
    소스에 연결한 설계 해석입니다. 자동 연결 검사는 의미 승인이나 기기 실행 검증이 아닙니다.
    
    LLM 그림 초안 · 사람 검토 전
    
    작성 모델: `cli/codex/gpt-6.1-terra high`
    
    | 요소·전이 | 소스 근거 |
    |---|---|
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="Camera supplied to configure()">configure&#40;&#41;에 전달된 Camera</span> | [src/libcamera/pipeline/rkisp1/rkisp1.cpp:914](#source-c164eb4c00d1c1d6) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="Selected RkISP1CameraData">선택된 RkISP1CameraData</span> | [src/libcamera/pipeline/rkisp1/rkisp1.cpp:914](#source-c164eb4c00d1c1d6) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="Optional calibration value dewarpParams_">선택적 보정값 dewarpParams&#95;</span> | [src/libcamera/pipeline/rkisp1/rkisp1.cpp:94](#source-04546183f68b35f4) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="Dewarp entry in the tuning file">튜닝 파일의 Dewarp 항목</span> | [src/libcamera/pipeline/rkisp1/rkisp1.cpp:434](#source-af6210cb39d0385e) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="loadDewarpParams()">loadDewarpParams&#40;&#41;</span> | [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:105](#source-eacff078bd6a9d79) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="Dewarper configuration in a separate configure(). Arrows show ownership and data transfer, not execution order.">별도 configure&#40;&#41;의 보정기 설정. 화살표는 소유권과 데이터 전달을 나타내며 실행 순서를 뜻하지 않습니다.</span> | [src/libcamera/pipeline/rkisp1/rkisp1.cpp:914](#source-c164eb4c00d1c1d6) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="configure(camera) selects the camera data with cameraData(camera).">configure&#40;camera&#41;가 cameraData&#40;camera&#41;로 카메라 데이터를 선택합니다.</span> | [src/libcamera/pipeline/rkisp1/rkisp1.cpp:914](#source-c164eb4c00d1c1d6) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="The camera data contains the optional calibration value.">카메라 데이터에 선택적 보정값이 포함됩니다.</span> | [src/libcamera/pipeline/rkisp1/rkisp1.cpp:94](#source-04546183f68b35f4) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="The Dewarp entry is passed as params.">Dewarp 항목을 params로 전달합니다.</span> | [src/libcamera/pipeline/rkisp1/rkisp1.cpp:434](#source-af6210cb39d0385e) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="The loader resets the camera&#x27;s optional by reference and stores values when all required validation passes.">로더는 카메라의 선택적 객체를 참조로 받아 비우고, 필수 검증을 모두 통과하면 값을 저장합니다.</span> | [src/libcamera/pipeline/rkisp1/rkisp1.cpp:434](#source-af6210cb39d0385e) · [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:105](#source-eacff078bd6a9d79) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="If cm, coefficients and cmNew are all absent, the loader succeeds with the optional empty.">cm, coefficients, cmNew가 모두 없으면 객체를 비운 채 성공합니다.</span> | [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:105](#source-eacff078bd6a9d79) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="Separate configure() passes the possibly empty optional upon reaching the main path with usesDewarper_ true.">별도 configure&#40;&#41;가 메인 경로에 도달하고 usesDewarper&#95;가 true이면 비어 있을 수도 있는 객체를 전달합니다.</span> | [src/libcamera/pipeline/rkisp1/rkisp1.cpp:914](#source-c164eb4c00d1c1d6) · [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:105](#source-eacff078bd6a9d79) |
<!-- /sdd:visual:per-camera-parameters -->

`RkISP1CameraData::loadTuningFile()`은 보정기가 없으면 파일을 열지 않고 0을 반환합니다. 파일 열기에 실패하면 파일 오류를 반환하고, YAML 파싱에 실패하면 `-EINVAL`을 반환합니다. `modules`가 없거나 목록에 `Dewarp` 항목이 없으면 0을 반환합니다. `src/libcamera/pipeline/rkisp1/rkisp1.cpp:434`

`PipelineHandlerRkISP1::configure()`는 현재 `camera`에서 `cameraData(camera)`를 가져와 `canUseDewarper_`가 참이고 첫 스트림이 RAW가 아닐 때 `usesDewarper_`를 참으로 설정합니다. 메인 경로 스트림에서 보정기를 사용하면 `dewarper_->configure()`에 현재 카메라 데이터의 `dewarpParams_`를 전달하고, 반환값이 0이 아니면 그 값을 반환합니다. `src/libcamera/pipeline/rkisp1/rkisp1.cpp:914`

근거 원문: [src/libcamera/pipeline/rkisp1/rkisp1.cpp:94](#source-04546183f68b35f4) · [src/libcamera/pipeline/rkisp1/rkisp1.cpp:434](#source-af6210cb39d0385e) · [src/libcamera/pipeline/rkisp1/rkisp1.cpp:914](#source-c164eb4c00d1c1d6)

## 튜닝 입력의 기본값과 오류

`ConverterDW100Module::loadDewarpParams`는 먼저 `dewarpParams`를 비웁니다. `cm`, `coefficients`, `cmNew`가 모두 없으면 `0`을 반환합니다. 하나라도 있으면 `cm`을 3×3 `double` 행렬로 읽어 유효한 값을 `dp.cm`에 저장한 뒤, `coefficients`를 `double` 목록으로 읽어 `setCoefficients`에 넘깁니다. `cm` 또는 `coefficients`가 없거나 읽기에 실패하거나 `setCoefficients`가 오류를 반환하면 `-EINVAL`을 반환합니다. `src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:105`

<!-- /sdd:visual:item-parameters -->

`Dw100VertexMap::DewarpParams::setCoefficients`는 계수의 개수가 4, 5, 8, 12개인지 `if (!(s == 4 || s == 5 || s == 8 || s == 12))`로 검사하고, 다른 개수이면 `-EINVAL`을 반환합니다. `loadDewarpParams`는 `setCoefficients`의 반환값이 0이 아니면 계수가 4개, 5개, 8개 또는 12개여야 한다는 오류를 기록하고 `-EINVAL`을 반환합니다. `src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100_vertexmap.cpp:258`, `src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:105`

`ConverterDW100Module::loadDewarpParams`는 앞선 검사를 통과한 뒤 `cmNew`가 있으면 이를 3×3 `double` 행렬로 읽어 `dp.cmNew`에 저장하며, 변환에 실패하면 `-EINVAL`을 반환합니다. `cmNew`가 없으면 `dp.cm`을 `dp.cmNew`에 복사합니다. 두 경로에서 처리가 성공하면 `dp`를 `dewarpParams`에 저장한 뒤 `0`을 반환합니다. `src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:105`

근거 원문: [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:105](#source-eacff078bd6a9d79) · [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100_vertexmap.cpp:258](#source-abfd8a7335a891ed)

## 스트림 적용과 실행 확인 범위

`ConverterDW100Module::configure`는 각 출력 설정을 순회하며 해당 스트림의 `vertexMap`에 `inputCfg.size`를 `setInputSize`로, `outputCfg.size`를 `setOutputSize`로, `sensorCrop_`을 `setSensorCrop`으로 순서대로 전달합니다. `ConverterDW100Module::configure`는 출력 스트림을 순회하면서 `dewarpParams`에 값이 있으면 해당 스트림의 `vertexMap.setDewarpParams`를 호출하고 `*dewarpParams`를 전달합니다. `src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:178`

??? note "그림 근거와 확인 범위"
    ConverterDW100Module::configure는 기저 구성 결과에 따라 무엇을 반환하며 각 출력의 맵을 어떻게 초기화합니까?
    
    소스에 연결한 설계 해석입니다. 자동 연결 검사는 의미 승인이나 기기 실행 검증이 아닙니다.

??? note "그림 근거와 확인 범위"
    updateControlInfos는 스트림 구성과 보정값에 따라 지원 제어를 어떻게 채우며 요청 미지원 때 무엇을 합니까?
    
    소스에 연결한 설계 해석입니다. 자동 연결 검사는 의미 승인이나 기기 실행 검증이 아닙니다.

    | <span data-sdd-label-en="Is the lensDewarpEnable optional present? It can be present with a false bool value.">lensDewarpEnable optional이 존재합니까? 포함된 bool 값이 false여도 존재할 수 있습니다.</span> | [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400](#source-3b37038ea14e4a3f) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="It calls setLensDewarpEnable(*lensDewarpEnable) and sets info.update to true.">setLensDewarpEnable&#40;&#42;lensDewarpEnable&#41;을 호출하고 info.update를 true로 설정합니다.</span> | [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400](#source-3b37038ea14e4a3f) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="Is the crop optional for ScalerCrop present?">ScalerCrop의 crop optional이 존재합니까?</span> | [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400](#source-3b37038ea14e4a3f) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="It calls setScalerCrop(*crop) and sets info.update to true.">setScalerCrop&#40;&#42;crop&#41;을 호출하고 info.update를 true로 설정합니다.</span> | [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400](#source-3b37038ea14e4a3f) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="Is info.update true, running_ true, and request support absent from converter_?">info.update가 true이고 running&#95;이 참이며 converter&#95;가 요청을 지원하지 않습니까?</span> | [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400](#source-3b37038ea14e4a3f) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="It logs that a dw100 kernel driver with requests support is required. It does not revert earlier updates.">요청을 지원하는 dw100 커널 드라이버가 필요하다는 오류를 LOG로 기록합니다. 앞서 갱신한 값은 되돌리지 않습니다.</span> | [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400](#source-3b37038ea14e4a3f) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="The function ends.">함수가 끝납니다.</span> | [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400](#source-3b37038ea14e4a3f) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="It checks whether stream is not configured.">stream의 미구성 여부를 검사합니다.</span> | [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400](#source-3b37038ea14e4a3f) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="If the condition is true, it returns immediately. · !isConfigured(stream) [true]">조건이 참이면 즉시 반환합니다. · &#33;isConfigured&#40;stream&#41; &#91;true&#93;</span> | [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400](#source-3b37038ea14e4a3f) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="If the condition is false, it checks the lensDewarpEnable optional. · !isConfigured(stream) [false]">조건이 거짓이면 lensDewarpEnable optional을 검사합니다. · &#33;isConfigured&#40;stream&#41; &#91;false&#93;</span> | [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400](#source-3b37038ea14e4a3f) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="If the optional is present, it passes the value, including false. · lensDewarpEnable [true]">optional이 존재하면 false를 포함한 값을 전달합니다. · lensDewarpEnable &#91;true&#93;</span> | [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400](#source-3b37038ea14e4a3f) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="If the optional is absent, it checks crop. · lensDewarpEnable [false]">optional이 없으면 crop을 검사합니다. · lensDewarpEnable &#91;false&#93;</span> | [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400](#source-3b37038ea14e4a3f) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="After the call returns normally and the update is set, it checks crop.">호출이 정상 복귀하고 갱신한 뒤 crop을 검사합니다.</span> | [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400](#source-3b37038ea14e4a3f) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="If the optional is present, it passes the value. · crop [true]">optional이 존재하면 값을 전달합니다. · crop &#91;true&#93;</span> | [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400](#source-3b37038ea14e4a3f) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="If the optional is absent, it checks the request support error condition. · crop [false]">optional이 없으면 요청 미지원 오류 조건을 검사합니다. · crop &#91;false&#93;</span> | [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400](#source-3b37038ea14e4a3f) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="After the call returns normally and the update is set, it checks the error condition.">호출이 정상 복귀하고 갱신한 뒤 오류 조건을 검사합니다.</span> | [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400](#source-3b37038ea14e4a3f) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="If the condition is true, it logs an error. · info.update &amp;&amp; running_ &amp;&amp; !converter_.supportsRequests() [true]">조건이 참이면 오류를 기록합니다. · info.update &amp;&amp; running&#95; &amp;&amp; &#33;converter&#95;.supportsRequests&#40;&#41; &#91;true&#93;</span> | [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400](#source-3b37038ea14e4a3f) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="If the condition is false, the function ends. · info.update &amp;&amp; running_ &amp;&amp; !converter_.supportsRequests() [false]">조건이 거짓이면 함수가 끝납니다. · info.update &amp;&amp; running&#95; &amp;&amp; &#33;converter&#95;.supportsRequests&#40;&#41; &#91;false&#93;</span> | [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400](#source-3b37038ea14e4a3f) |
    | <span data-sdd-label-en="The function ends when logging the error returns normally.">오류 기록이 정상 복귀하면 함수가 끝납니다.</span> | [src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400](#source-3b37038ea14e4a3f) |

<!-- /sdd:visual:main-path-dewarp-handoff -->

`ConverterDW100Module::updateControlInfos`는 `hasDewarpParams_`가 참이면 `controls::LensDewarpEnable`을 지원 제어로 등록하고 제어 정보의 최솟값, 최댓값, 기본값을 각각 `false`, `true`, `true`로 설정합니다. `ConverterDW100Module::setControls`는 `isConfigured(stream)`이 거짓이면 반환하고 종료합니다. 구성된 스트림에서 `ConverterDW100Module::setControls`는 `info.update`와 `running_`이 참이고 `converter_.supportsRequests()`가 거짓이면 동적 제어에 관한 오류 로그를 기록합니다. `populateMetadata`는 `isConfigured(stream)` 검사에서 스트림이 설정되지 않았으면 즉시 반환합니다. `src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:369`, `src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:402`, `src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400`, `src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:434`

`ConverterDW100Module::populateMetadata`는 구성된 스트림의 `vertexMap.effectiveScalerCrop()` 값을 `controls::ScalerCrop`에 기록합니다. 이어서 `hasDewarpParams_`가 참이면 `vertexMap.lensDewarpEnable()` 값을 `controls::LensDewarpEnable`에 기록합니다. `src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:441`, `src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:443`

??? note "소스 근거: src/libcamera/pipeline/rkisp1/rkisp1.cpp:434"
    `src/libcamera/pipeline/rkisp1/rkisp1.cpp:434`에서 시작하는 발췌입니다. 종료 줄은 479이며, 아래 원문을 설명과 대조할 수 있습니다.
    
    SHA-256: `d0fb5eb90b4c6d0df3e90cff142c95b4f7daeddb6d483a4272a5fb07d7dd3fec`
    
    ```text
    int RkISP1CameraData::loadTuningFile(const std::string &path)
    {
    	int ret;
    
    	if (!pipe()->dewarper_)
    		/* Nothing to do without dewarper */
    		return 0;
    
    	LOG(RkISP1, Debug) << "Load tuning file " << path;
    
    	File file(path);
    	if (!file.open(File::OpenModeFlag::ReadOnly)) {
    		ret = file.error();
    		LOG(RkISP1, Error)
    			<< "Failed to open tuning file "
    			<< path << ": " << strerror(-ret);
    		return ret;
    	}
    
    	std::unique_ptr<ValueNode> data = YamlParser::parse(file);
    	if (!data)
    		return -EINVAL;
    
    	if (!data->contains("modules"))
    		return 0;
    
    	const auto &modules = (*data)["modules"].asList();
    	for (const auto &module : modules) {
    		const auto &params = module["Dewarp"];
    		if (!params)
    			continue;
    
    		ret = pipe()->dewarper_->loadDewarpParams(params, dewarpParams_);
    		if (ret)
    			return ret;
    
    		LOG(RkISP1, Info) << "Dw100 dewarper initialized";
    
    		canUseDewarper_ = true;
    		return 0;
    	}
    
    	return 0;
    }
    ```

??? note "소스 근거: src/libcamera/pipeline/rkisp1/rkisp1.cpp:914"
    `src/libcamera/pipeline/rkisp1/rkisp1.cpp:914`에서 시작하는 발췌입니다. 종료 줄은 1122이며, 아래 원문을 설명과 대조할 수 있습니다.
    
    SHA-256: `85e3aef5f0b623538bedbd13a895c0a08ada48616ab1e9ee8fe1a87882b67ee6`
    
    ```text
    int PipelineHandlerRkISP1::configure(Camera *camera, CameraConfiguration *c)
    {
    	RkISP1CameraConfiguration *config =
    		static_cast<RkISP1CameraConfiguration *>(c);
    	RkISP1CameraData *data = cameraData(camera);
    	CameraSensor *sensor = data->sensor_.get();
    	int ret;
    
    	ret = initLinks(camera, *config);
    	if (ret)
    		return ret;
    
    	const PixelFormat &streamFormat = config->at(0).pixelFormat;
    	const PixelFormatInfo &info = PixelFormatInfo::info(streamFormat);
    	isRaw_ = info.colourEncoding == PixelFormatInfo::ColourEncodingRAW;
    	data->usesDewarper_ = data->canUseDewarper_ && !isRaw_;
    
    	Transform transform = config->combinedTransform();
    	bool transposeAfterIsp = false;
    	if (data->usesDewarper_) {
    		if (!!(transform & Transform::Transpose))
    			transposeAfterIsp = true;
    		transform = Transform::Identity;
    	}
    
    	/*
    	 * Configure the format on the sensor output and propagate it through
    	 * the pipeline.
    	 */
    	V4L2SubdeviceFormat format = config->sensorFormat();
    	LOG(RkISP1, Debug) << "Configuring sensor with " << format;
    
    	if (config->sensorConfig)
    		ret = sensor->applyConfiguration(*config->sensorConfig,
    						 transform,
    						 &format);
    	else
    		ret = sensor->setFormat(&format, transform);
    
    	if (ret < 0)
    		return ret;
    
    	LOG(RkISP1, Debug) << "Sensor configured with " << format;
    
    	/* Propagate format through the internal media pipeline up to the ISP */
    	ret = data->pipe_.configure(sensor, &format);
    	if (ret < 0)
    		return ret;
    
    	LOG(RkISP1, Debug) << "Configuring ISP with : " << format;
    	ret = isp_->setFormat(0, &format);
    	if (ret < 0)
    		return ret;
    
    	Rectangle inputCrop(0, 0, format.size);
    	ret = isp_->setSelection(0, V4L2_SEL_TGT_CROP, &inputCrop);
    	if (ret < 0)
    		return ret;
    
    	LOG(RkISP1, Debug)
    		<< "ISP input pad configured with " << format
    		<< " crop " << inputCrop;
    
    	Rectangle outputCrop = inputCrop;
    
    	/* YUYV8_2X8 is required on the ISP source path pad for YUV output. */
    	if (!isRaw_)
    		format.code = MEDIA_BUS_FMT_YUYV8_2X8;
    
    	/*
    	 * On devices without DUAL_CROP (like the imx8mp) cropping needs to be
    	 * done on the ISP/IS output.
    	 *
    	 * If the dewarper is used, the cropping shall be done by the dewarper.
    	 */
    	if (media_->hwRevision() == RKISP1_V_IMX8MP) {
    		/* imx8mp has only a single path. */
    		const auto &cfg = config->at(0);
    		/*
    		 * If the dewarper is used, all cropping including aspect ratio
    		 * preservation shall be done there. To ensure that the output
    		 * format provided by the ISP is supported by the dewarper, a
    		 * minimal crop still needs to be applied on the ISP output.
    		 *
    		 * \todo It might be possible to allocate bigger buffers
    		 * (aligned to 8 pixels) with a stride matching format.size for
    		 * the ISP. The not-filled border could later be ignored by the
    		 * dewarper. This way we could skip the minimal crop here and
    		 * the MaximumScalerCrop would always match the isp output.
    		 */
    		Size ispCrop;
    		if (data->usesDewarper_)
    			ispCrop = dewarper_->adjustInputSize(cfg.pixelFormat,
    							     format.size);
    		else
    			ispCrop = format.size.boundedToAspectRatio(cfg.size)
    					  .alignedUpTo(2, 2);
    
    		outputCrop = ispCrop.centeredTo(Rectangle(format.size).center());
    		format.size = ispCrop;
    	}
    
    	LOG(RkISP1, Debug)
    		<< "Configuring ISP output pad with " << format
    		<< " crop " << outputCrop;
    
    	ret = isp_->setSelection(2, V4L2_SEL_TGT_CROP, &outputCrop);
    	if (ret < 0)
    		return ret;
    
    	format.colorSpace = config->at(0).colorSpace;
    	ret = isp_->setFormat(2, &format);
    	if (ret < 0)
    		return ret;
    
    	LOG(RkISP1, Debug)
    		<< "ISP output pad configured with " << format
    		<< " crop " << outputCrop;
    
    	IPACameraSensorInfo sensorInfo;
    	ret = data->sensor_->sensorInfo(&sensorInfo);
    	if (ret)
    		return ret;
    
    	/* Apply the actual sensor crop for proper dewarp map calculation. */
    	Rectangle sensorCrop = outputCrop.transformedBetween(
    		inputCrop, sensorInfo.analogCrop);
    	if (data->usesDewarper_)
    		dewarper_->setSensorCrop(sensorCrop);
    	data->properties_.set(properties::ScalerCropMaximum, sensorCrop);
    
    	std::map<unsigned int, IPAStream> streamConfig;
    
    	for (const StreamConfiguration &cfg : *config) {
    		if (cfg.stream() == &data->mainPathStream_) {
    			/*
    			 * To allow for digital zoom, scaling down should happen
    			 * in the dewarper, instead of the resizer. Configure
    			 * the isp output to the same size as the sensor output.
    			 */
    			StreamConfiguration ispCfg = cfg;
    			if (data->usesDewarper_) {
    				ispCfg.bufferCount = kRkISP1MinBufferCount;
    				ispCfg.size = format.size;
    				ispCfg.stride =
    					PixelFormatInfo::info(ispCfg.pixelFormat)
    						.stride(ispCfg.size.width, 0);
    
    				ret = dewarper_->configure(ispCfg, { cfg }, data->dewarpParams_);
    				if (ret)
    					return ret;
    
    				dewarper_->setTransform(cfg.stream(), config->combinedTransform());
    				/*
    				 * Apply a default scaler crop that keeps the
    				 * aspect ratio.
    				 */
    				Size size = cfg.size;
    				if (transposeAfterIsp)
    					size.transpose();
    				size = sensorCrop.size().boundedToAspectRatio(size);
    
    				ControlList ctrls;
    				ctrls.set(controls::ScalerCrop, size.centeredTo(sensorCrop.center()));
    				dewarper_->setControls(cfg.stream(), ctrls);
    			}
    
    			ret = mainPath_.configure(ispCfg, format);
    			streamConfig[0] = IPAStream(cfg.pixelFormat,
    						    cfg.size);
    		} else if (hasSelfPath_) {
    			ret = selfPath_.configure(cfg, format);
    			streamConfig[1] = IPAStream(cfg.pixelFormat,
    						    cfg.size);
    		} else {
    			return -ENODEV;
    		}
    
    		if (ret)
    			return ret;
    	}
    
    	V4L2DeviceFormat paramFormat;
    	paramFormat.fourcc = V4L2PixelFormat(V4L2_META_FMT_RK_ISP1_EXT_PARAMS);
    	ret = param_->setFormat(&paramFormat);
    	if (ret)
    		return ret;
    
    	V4L2DeviceFormat statFormat;
    	statFormat.fourcc = V4L2PixelFormat(V4L2_META_FMT_RK_ISP1_STAT_3A);
    	ret = stat_->setFormat(&statFormat);
    	if (ret)
    		return ret;
    
    	/* Inform IPA of stream configuration and sensor controls. */
    	ipa::rkisp1::IPAConfigInfo ipaConfig{ sensorInfo,
    					      data->sensor_->controls(),
    					      paramFormat.fourcc };
    
    	ret = data->ipa_->configure(ipaConfig, streamConfig, &data->ipaControls_);
    	if (ret) {
    		LOG(RkISP1, Error) << "failed configuring IPA (" << ret << ")";
    		return ret;
    	}
    
    	return updateControls(data);
    }
    ```

    SHA-256: `5e4f1317cd9611173e6e5b1fa350ab26f28500656037c812909008604ebfb091`
    
    ```text
    int ConverterDW100Module::loadDewarpParams(const ValueNode &params,
    					   std::optional<Dw100VertexMap::DewarpParams> &dewarpParams)
    {
    	Dw100VertexMap::DewarpParams dp;
    	dewarpParams.reset();
    
    	auto &cm = params["cm"];
    	auto &coefficients = params["coefficients"];
    	auto &cmNew = params["cmNew"];
    
    	/* If nothing is provided, the dewarper is still functional */
    	if (!cm && !coefficients && !cmNew)
    		return 0;
    
    	if (!cm) {
    		LOG(Converter, Error) << "Dewarp parameters are missing 'cm' value";
    		return -EINVAL;
    	}
    
    	auto matrix = cm.get<Matrix<double, 3, 3>>();
    	if (!matrix) {
    		LOG(Converter, Error) << "Failed to load 'cm' value";
    		return -EINVAL;
    	}
    
    	dp.cm = *matrix;
    
    	if (!coefficients) {
    		LOG(Converter, Error) << "Dewarp parameters are missing 'coefficients' value";
    		return -EINVAL;
    	}
    
    	const auto coeffs = coefficients.get<std::vector<double>>();
    	if (!coeffs) {
    		LOG(Converter, Error) << "Dewarp parameters 'coefficients' value is not a list";
    		return -EINVAL;
    	}
    
    	int ret = dp.setCoefficients(*coeffs);
    	if (ret) {
    		LOG(Converter, Error)
    			<< "Dewarp 'coefficients' must have 4, 5, 8 or 12 values";
    		return -EINVAL;
    	}
    
    	if (cmNew) {
    		matrix = cmNew.get<Matrix<double, 3, 3>>();
    		if (!matrix) {
    			LOG(Converter, Error) << "Failed to load 'cmNew' value";
    			return -EINVAL;
    		}
    
    		dp.cmNew = *matrix;
    	} else {
    		dp.cmNew = dp.cm;
    	}
    
    	dewarpParams = dp;
    
    	return 0;
    }
    ```

    `src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100_vertexmap.cpp:258`에서 시작하는 발췌입니다. 종료 줄은 290이며, 아래 원문을 설명과 대조할 수 있습니다.
    
    SHA-256: `b1a17e4f1ba9764bfd619a46ec1e5089c0120986720f4300e39d0723259d1b20`
    
    ```text
    int Dw100VertexMap::DewarpParams::setCoefficients(std::span<const double> coeffs)
    {
    	size_t s = coeffs.size();
    	if (!(s == 4 || s == 5 || s == 8 || s == 12))
    		return -EINVAL;
    
    	coefficients = {};
    
    	if (s >= 4) {
    		coefficients.k1 = coeffs[0];
    		coefficients.k2 = coeffs[1];
    		coefficients.p1 = coeffs[2];
    		coefficients.p2 = coeffs[3];
    	}
    
    	if (s >= 5)
    		coefficients.k3 = coeffs[4];
    
    	if (s >= 8) {
    		coefficients.k4 = coeffs[5];
    		coefficients.k5 = coeffs[6];
    		coefficients.k6 = coeffs[7];
    	}
    
    	if (s == 12) {
    		coefficients.s1 = coeffs[8];
    		coefficients.s2 = coeffs[9];
    		coefficients.s3 = coeffs[10];
    		coefficients.s4 = coeffs[11];
    	}
    
    	return 0;
    }
    ```

    `src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:178`에서 시작하는 발췌입니다. 종료 줄은 212이며, 아래 원문을 설명과 대조할 수 있습니다.
    
    SHA-256: `b517243a55de2dab95250c687c7f2aab9fd32b4ed115e3b65b8878daa39b0bc8`
    
    ```text
    int ConverterDW100Module::configure(const StreamConfiguration &inputCfg,
    				    const std::vector<std::reference_wrapper<const StreamConfiguration>>
    					    &outputCfgs,
    				    const std::optional<Dw100VertexMap::DewarpParams> &dewarpParams)
    {
    	int ret;
    
    	vertexMaps_.clear();
    	ret = converter_.configure(inputCfg, outputCfgs);
    	if (ret)
    		return ret;
    
    	inputBufferCount_ = inputCfg.bufferCount;
    	hasDewarpParams_ = dewarpParams.has_value();
    
    	for (auto &ref : outputCfgs) {
    		const auto &outputCfg = ref.get();
    		auto &info = vertexMaps_[outputCfg.stream()];
    		auto &vertexMap = info.map;
    		vertexMap.setInputSize(inputCfg.size);
    		vertexMap.setOutputSize(outputCfg.size);
    		vertexMap.setSensorCrop(sensorCrop_);
    
    		if (dewarpParams)
    			vertexMap.setDewarpParams(*dewarpParams);
    		info.update = true;
    	}
    
    	return 0;
    }
    ```

    ```text
    void ConverterDW100Module::updateControlInfos(const Stream *stream, ControlInfoMap::Map &controls)
    {
    	ControlValue scalerCropDefault = sensorCrop_;
    
    	if (isConfigured(stream)) {
    		auto &info = vertexMaps_[stream];
    		info.map.applyLimits();
    		scalerCropDefault = info.map.effectiveScalerCrop();
    	}
    
    	controls[&controls::ScalerCrop] = ControlInfo(Rectangle(sensorCrop_.x, sensorCrop_.y, 1, 1),
    						      sensorCrop_, sensorCrop_);
    
    	if (hasDewarpParams_)
    		controls[&controls::LensDewarpEnable] = ControlInfo(false, true, true);
    
    	if (!converter_.supportsRequests())
    		LOG(Converter, Warning)
    			<< "dw100 kernel driver has no requests support."
    			   " Dynamic configuration is not possible.";
    }
    
    /**
     * \brief Set libcamera controls
     * \param[in] stream The stream to update
     * \param[in] controls The controls
     *
     * Looks up all supported controls in \a controls and sets them on stream \a
     * stream. The controls will be applied to the device on the next call to
     * queueBuffers().
     */
    void ConverterDW100Module::setControls(const Stream *stream, const ControlList &controls)
    {
    	if (!isConfigured(stream))
    		return;
    
    	auto &info = vertexMaps_[stream];
    	auto &vertexMap = info.map;
    
    	const auto &lensDewarpEnable = controls.get(controls::LensDewarpEnable);
    	if (lensDewarpEnable) {
    		vertexMap.setLensDewarpEnable(*lensDewarpEnable);
    		info.update = true;
    	}
    
    	const auto &crop = controls.get(controls::ScalerCrop);
    	if (crop) {
    		vertexMap.setScalerCrop(*crop);
    		info.update = true;
    	}
    
    	if (info.update && running_ && !converter_.supportsRequests())
    		LOG(Converter, Error)
    			<< "Dynamically setting dw100 specific controls requires"
    			   " a dw100 kernel driver with requests support";
    }
    ```

    ```text
    void ConverterDW100Module::populateMetadata(const Stream *stream, ControlList &meta)
    {
    	if (!isConfigured(stream))
    		return;
    
    	auto &vertexMap = vertexMaps_[stream].map;
    
    	meta.set(controls::ScalerCrop, vertexMap.effectiveScalerCrop());
    
    	if (hasDewarpParams_)
    		meta.set(controls::LensDewarpEnable, vertexMap.lensDewarpEnable());
    }
    ```

    ```text
    int PipelineHandlerRkISP1::configure(Camera *camera, CameraConfiguration *c)
    {
    	RkISP1CameraConfiguration *config =
    		static_cast<RkISP1CameraConfiguration *>(c);
    	RkISP1CameraData *data = cameraData(camera);
    	CameraSensor *sensor = data->sensor_.get();
    	int ret;
    
    	ret = initLinks(camera, *config);
    	if (ret)
    		return ret;
    
    	const PixelFormat &streamFormat = config->at(0).pixelFormat;
    	const PixelFormatInfo &info = PixelFormatInfo::info(streamFormat);
    	isRaw_ = info.colourEncoding == PixelFormatInfo::ColourEncodingRAW;
    	data->usesDewarper_ = data->canUseDewarper_ && !isRaw_;
    
    	Transform transform = config->combinedTransform();
    	bool transposeAfterIsp = false;
    	if (data->usesDewarper_) {
    		if (!!(transform & Transform::Transpose))
    			transposeAfterIsp = true;
    		transform = Transform::Identity;
    	}
    
    	/*
    	 * Configure the format on the sensor output and propagate it through
    	 * the pipeline.
    	 */
    	V4L2SubdeviceFormat format = config->sensorFormat();
    	LOG(RkISP1, Debug) << "Configuring sensor with " << format;
    
    	if (config->sensorConfig)
    		ret = sensor->applyConfiguration(*config->sensorConfig,
    						 transform,
    						 &format);
    	else
    		ret = sensor->setFormat(&format, transform);
    
    	if (ret < 0)
    		return ret;
    
    	LOG(RkISP1, Debug) << "Sensor configured with " << format;
    
    	/* Propagate format through the internal media pipeline up to the ISP */
    	ret = data->pipe_.configure(sensor, &format);
    	if (ret < 0)
    		return ret;
    
    	LOG(RkISP1, Debug) << "Configuring ISP with : " << format;
    	ret = isp_->setFormat(0, &format);
    	if (ret < 0)
    		return ret;
    
    	Rectangle inputCrop(0, 0, format.size);
    	ret = isp_->setSelection(0, V4L2_SEL_TGT_CROP, &inputCrop);
    	if (ret < 0)
    		return ret;
    
    	LOG(RkISP1, Debug)
    		<< "ISP input pad configured with " << format
    		<< " crop " << inputCrop;
    
    	Rectangle outputCrop = inputCrop;
    
    	/* YUYV8_2X8 is required on the ISP source path pad for YUV output. */
    	if (!isRaw_)
    		format.code = MEDIA_BUS_FMT_YUYV8_2X8;
    
    	/*
    	 * On devices without DUAL_CROP (like the imx8mp) cropping needs to be
    	 * done on the ISP/IS output.
    	 *
    	 * If the dewarper is used, the cropping shall be done by the dewarper.
    	 */
    	if (media_->hwRevision() == RKISP1_V_IMX8MP) {
    		/* imx8mp has only a single path. */
    		const auto &cfg = config->at(0);
    		/*
    		 * If the dewarper is used, all cropping including aspect ratio
    		 * preservation shall be done there. To ensure that the output
    		 * format provided by the ISP is supported by the dewarper, a
    		 * minimal crop still needs to be applied on the ISP output.
    		 *
    		 * \todo It might be possible to allocate bigger buffers
    		 * (aligned to 8 pixels) with a stride matching format.size for
    		 * the ISP. The not-filled border could later be ignored by the
    		 * dewarper. This way we could skip the minimal crop here and
    		 * the MaximumScalerCrop would always match the isp output.
    		 */
    		Size ispCrop;
    		if (data->usesDewarper_)
    			ispCrop = dewarper_->adjustInputSize(cfg.pixelFormat,
    							     format.size);
    		else
    			ispCrop = format.size.boundedToAspectRatio(cfg.size)
    					  .alignedUpTo(2, 2);
    
    		outputCrop = ispCrop.centeredTo(Rectangle(format.size).center());
    		format.size = ispCrop;
    	}
    
    	LOG(RkISP1, Debug)
    		<< "Configuring ISP output pad with " << format
    		<< " crop " << outputCrop;
    
    	ret = isp_->setSelection(2, V4L2_SEL_TGT_CROP, &outputCrop);
    	if (ret < 0)
    		return ret;
    
    	format.colorSpace = config->at(0).colorSpace;
    	ret = isp_->setFormat(2, &format);
    	if (ret < 0)
    		return ret;
    
    	LOG(RkISP1, Debug)
    		<< "ISP output pad configured with " << format
    		<< " crop " << outputCrop;
    
    	IPACameraSensorInfo sensorInfo;
    	ret = data->sensor_->sensorInfo(&sensorInfo);
    	if (ret)
    		return ret;
    
    	/* Apply the actual sensor crop for proper dewarp map calculation. */
    	Rectangle sensorCrop = outputCrop.transformedBetween(
    		inputCrop, sensorInfo.analogCrop);
    	if (data->usesDewarper_)
    		dewarper_->setSensorCrop(sensorCrop);
    	data->properties_.set(properties::ScalerCropMaximum, sensorCrop);
    
    	std::map<unsigned int, IPAStream> streamConfig;
    
    	for (const StreamConfiguration &cfg : *config) {
    		if (cfg.stream() == &data->mainPathStream_) {
    			/*
    			 * To allow for digital zoom, scaling down should happen
    			 * in the dewarper, instead of the resizer. Configure
    			 * the isp output to the same size as the sensor output.
    			 */
    			StreamConfiguration ispCfg = cfg;
    			if (data->usesDewarper_) {
    				ispCfg.bufferCount = kRkISP1MinBufferCount;
    				ispCfg.size = format.size;
    				ispCfg.stride =
    					PixelFormatInfo::info(ispCfg.pixelFormat)
    						.stride(ispCfg.size.width, 0);
    
    				ret = dewarper_->configure(ispCfg, { cfg }, data->dewarpParams_);
    				if (ret)
    					return ret;
    
    				dewarper_->setTransform(cfg.stream(), config->combinedTransform());
    				/*
    				 * Apply a default scaler crop that keeps the
    				 * aspect ratio.
    				 */
    				Size size = cfg.size;
    				if (transposeAfterIsp)
    					size.transpose();
    				size = sensorCrop.size().boundedToAspectRatio(size);
    
    				ControlList ctrls;
    				ctrls.set(controls::ScalerCrop, size.centeredTo(sensorCrop.center()));
    				dewarper_->setControls(cfg.stream(), ctrls);
    			}
    
    			ret = mainPath_.configure(ispCfg, format);
    			streamConfig[0] = IPAStream(cfg.pixelFormat,
    						    cfg.size);
    		} else if (hasSelfPath_) {
    			ret = selfPath_.configure(cfg, format);
    			streamConfig[1] = IPAStream(cfg.pixelFormat,
    						    cfg.size);
    		} else {
    			return -ENODEV;
    		}
    
    		if (ret)
    			return ret;
    	}
    
    	V4L2DeviceFormat paramFormat;
    	paramFormat.fourcc = V4L2PixelFormat(V4L2_META_FMT_RK_ISP1_EXT_PARAMS);
    	ret = param_->setFormat(&paramFormat);
    	if (ret)
    		return ret;
    
    	V4L2DeviceFormat statFormat;
    	statFormat.fourcc = V4L2PixelFormat(V4L2_META_FMT_RK_ISP1_STAT_3A);
    	ret = stat_->setFormat(&statFormat);
    	if (ret)
    		return ret;
    
    	/* Inform IPA of stream configuration and sensor controls. */
    	ipa::rkisp1::IPAConfigInfo ipaConfig{ sensorInfo,
    					      data->sensor_->controls(),
    					      paramFormat.fourcc };
    
    	ret = data->ipa_->configure(ipaConfig, streamConfig, &data->ipaControls_);
    	if (ret) {
    		LOG(RkISP1, Error) << "failed configuring IPA (" << ret << ")";
    		return ret;
    	}
    
    	return updateControls(data);
    }
    ```

??? note "소스 근거: src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:178"
    `src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:178`에서 시작하는 발췌입니다. 종료 줄은 207이며, 아래 원문을 설명과 대조할 수 있습니다.
    
    SHA-256: `78dde0742228b34c3f439a9eddb6037b62427aabefc6d5d1cf1cca5ebbb1be07`
    
    ```text
    int ConverterDW100Module::configure(const StreamConfiguration &inputCfg,
    				    const std::vector<std::reference_wrapper<const StreamConfiguration>>
    					    &outputCfgs,
    				    const std::optional<Dw100VertexMap::DewarpParams> &dewarpParams)
    {
    	int ret;
    
    	vertexMaps_.clear();
    	ret = converter_.configure(inputCfg, outputCfgs);
    	if (ret)
    		return ret;
    
    	inputBufferCount_ = inputCfg.bufferCount;
    	hasDewarpParams_ = dewarpParams.has_value();
    
    	for (auto &ref : outputCfgs) {
    		const auto &outputCfg = ref.get();
    		auto &info = vertexMaps_[outputCfg.stream()];
    		auto &vertexMap = info.map;
    		vertexMap.setInputSize(inputCfg.size);
    		vertexMap.setOutputSize(outputCfg.size);
    		vertexMap.setSensorCrop(sensorCrop_);
    
    		if (dewarpParams)
    			vertexMap.setDewarpParams(*dewarpParams);
    		info.update = true;
    	}
    
    	return 0;
    }
    ```

    ```text
    bool ConverterDW100Module::isConfigured(const Stream *stream) const
    {
    	return vertexMaps_.find(stream) != vertexMaps_.end();
    }
    ```

??? note "소스 근거: src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:369"
    `src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:369`에서 시작하는 발췌입니다. 종료 줄은 389이며, 아래 원문을 설명과 대조할 수 있습니다.
    
    SHA-256: `e1b29934e982572d53d59c39d6c378efd91d24852bbca5ca00612947e57d91eb`
    
    ```text
    void ConverterDW100Module::updateControlInfos(const Stream *stream, ControlInfoMap::Map &controls)
    {
    	ControlValue scalerCropDefault = sensorCrop_;
    
    	if (isConfigured(stream)) {
    		auto &info = vertexMaps_[stream];
    		info.map.applyLimits();
    		scalerCropDefault = info.map.effectiveScalerCrop();
    	}
    
    	controls[&controls::ScalerCrop] = ControlInfo(Rectangle(sensorCrop_.x, sensorCrop_.y, 1, 1),
    						      sensorCrop_, sensorCrop_);
    
    	if (hasDewarpParams_)
    		controls[&controls::LensDewarpEnable] = ControlInfo(false, true, true);
    
    	if (!converter_.supportsRequests())
    		LOG(Converter, Warning)
    			<< "dw100 kernel driver has no requests support."
    			   " Dynamic configuration is not possible.";
    }
    ```

<a id="source-3b37038ea14e4a3f"></a>

??? note "소스 근거: src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400"
    `src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:400`에서 시작하는 발췌입니다. 종료 줄은 424이며, 아래 원문을 설명과 대조할 수 있습니다.
    
    SHA-256: `9103f3c7e936f73ecac9cd744a6fd3d94f6e54e14542d2a4c13e6c417faa8cee`
    
    ```text
    void ConverterDW100Module::setControls(const Stream *stream, const ControlList &controls)
    {
    	if (!isConfigured(stream))
    		return;
    
    	auto &info = vertexMaps_[stream];
    	auto &vertexMap = info.map;
    
    	const auto &lensDewarpEnable = controls.get(controls::LensDewarpEnable);
    	if (lensDewarpEnable) {
    		vertexMap.setLensDewarpEnable(*lensDewarpEnable);
    		info.update = true;
    	}
    
    	const auto &crop = controls.get(controls::ScalerCrop);
    	if (crop) {
    		vertexMap.setScalerCrop(*crop);
    		info.update = true;
    	}
    
    	if (info.update && running_ && !converter_.supportsRequests())
    		LOG(Converter, Error)
    			<< "Dynamically setting dw100 specific controls requires"
    			   " a dw100 kernel driver with requests support";
    }
    ```

??? note "소스 근거: src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:434"
    `src/libcamera/converter/converter_dw100.cpp:434`에서 시작하는 발췌입니다. 종료 줄은 445이며, 아래 원문을 설명과 대조할 수 있습니다.
    
    SHA-256: `c3910ce8099422d783f718ab0fc0de073c5999dd6aa53dfef296cf33fbfb3255`
    
    ```text
    void ConverterDW100Module::populateMetadata(const Stream *stream, ControlList &meta)
    {
    	if (!isConfigured(stream))
    		return;
    
    	auto &vertexMap = vertexMaps_[stream].map;
    
    	meta.set(controls::ScalerCrop, vertexMap.effectiveScalerCrop());
    
    	if (hasDewarpParams_)
    		meta.set(controls::LensDewarpEnable, vertexMap.lensDewarpEnable());
    }
    ```
